# Graph Report - hermes-agent  (2026-08-16)

## Corpus Check
- 3678 files · ~5,992,161 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 64139 nodes · 157811 edges · 1408 communities (1256 shown, 152 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4139 edges (avg confidence: 0.63)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `253d20a2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
